## Absolute-value meaning, equations and complete inequalities

Demands: 8227:2020:M2:T1:O01:D01, 8227:2020:M2:T1:O01:D02, 8227:2020:M2:T1:O01:D03.

### Teaching across the books

YM introduces nonnegative physical error magnitudes before grouping/nested bars,then isolates the modulus and distinguishes positive,zero and negative targets. Goldie starts with a function-value prompt and develops graphical distance/inside-outside reasoning. Both compare two moduli;YM7Q1 asks two methods whereasGoldieExample18 reuses equality crossings in an inequality. GoldieExample16 reverses a supplied interval into a centre/radius modulus statement, a direction less prominent inYM.

### Examination comparison

Directly assessed NSSCAS parts: 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q3:answer, 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:a, 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:b, 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q11:b, 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q11:c, 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q2:answer.

Same-type textbook practice: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-single-constant-equation to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:a; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-variable-rhs-equation to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q2:answer; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-two-inequality to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q3:answer.

Structural branches: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:quadratic-inequality to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q3:answer — The source starts with a quadratic inequality; the exam must justify an equivalent inequality from two absolute values before selecting the interval. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:line-curve-numeric-intersection to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q11:b — The source substitutes a line into xy+18=0, solves a quadratic and returns both coordinate pairs. The exam intersects a line with an absolute-value graph, chooses the consistent branch and returns one symbolic pair. The source adds quadratic/two-pair recovery; the exam adds modulus-branch validity and a symbolic parameter. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:quadratic-completed-square-inequality-interval to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q3:answer — Try Now23(c) can reuse the established completed-square form to isolate (x-3)^2<16 and return one open interval. The target must solve an inequality between two absolute affine expressions. The source is easier scaffolded inner-interval practice; the target additionally preserves absolute-value/logarithmic domains or selects outer intervals. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:exponential-logarithmic-solve to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:b — The source directly logs one positive exponential equation. The exam must first reuse the earlier absolute-value branches, reject the negative exponential preimage and only then take logs. The source omits that modulus/positive-preimage filtering. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-exponential-hence-chain to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:b — The source Example34 solves its two-modulus algebraic equation then reuses the single positive root for 2^y and takes logs. The isolated target inherits one-modulus roots including a negative one, so it additionally rejects a nonpositive exponential preimage; the source full chain additionally solves its preparatory equation. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-exponential-hence-chain to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:b — Only source Example34(b) is compared. Both reuse the previous modulus roots as exponential values and take logs, but the source has one positive earlier value and no inadmissible value to reject; the target must reject the negative earlier root. The preparatory source (a) is excluded. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-positive-exponential-equation to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:b — The source 2|3^x-1|=3^x requires choosing its positive exponential intermediate and deriving its own absolute-value branches with a variable right side. The target inherits the earlier one-modulus solution set and only filters/recover its positive exponential preimage. The source adds unsupplied branch algebra. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-positive-exponential-equation to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:a — The book splits modulus cases with a positive exponential as both the inner quantity and the right-hand side, solves for that positive quantity and applies logarithms to recover x. Exam a splits an affine modulus against a fixed positive constant and returns affine roots; it has no positivity/logarithm recovery decision. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:exponential-absolute-exponent-recovery to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q2:answer — Source Try Now34 Q3(b) reuses a known positive value of 3^x, replaces x by |x| and returns both sign preimages. The target has shifted |1-x| in one exponent and variable x in the other; after equating bases it must solve |1-x|=3x with a nonnegative-right-side check. Constant absolute-exponent recovery is easier. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-single-constant-equation to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q1:b — The source solves one affine absolute value directly. The target must reuse its preceding roots as exponential values, reject any nonpositive intermediate and take logs to recover the exponent. These are additional target preimage decisions. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:modulus-single-constant-equation to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q11:b — The source has a fixed positive right side and two direct linear branches. The target find symbolic intersection coordinates of a modulus graph and a signed straight line, so its variable signed right side needs a nonnegativity/branch check and back-substitution for the ordinate. Demands without an NSSCAS part: none.

### Difficulty and grade descriptors

E10 includes modulus notation/equations;C10 andA11 support equations and inequalities with broader combinations. The main increase is sign logic:constant target versus variable RHS,two nonnegative sides versus a line,empty/all-real cases and coupled original-variable recovery. A fixed positive symbolic parameter may change the midpoint without adding numerical difficulty. Grade boundaries are qualitative here.

### Pitfalls and evidential limits

GoldieExample17 explicitly requires care before squaring a comparison with a potentially negative side;YMExample6 filters a negative-RHS candidate. YMTry12(f)key misses an all-real solution,Goldie1C7 reverses its half-line,andYMExample12 has faulty equality-check prose. These registered source defects justify independent original-relation tests. No matching specific modulus learner-error frequency is claimed from the examiner register.

### Gaps and representations

Both books offer routine and two-modulus branches;Goldie provides inverse interval representation,YM negative/zero targets and explicit number lines. YMExercise10 asks coordinate pairs,Try26Q2 asks new output values,andRevision8Q4 asks bounded cotangent recovery. Their shared topic does not make their response evidence equivalent.

Demands: 8227:2020:M2:T1:O02:D01, 8227:2020:M2:T1:O02:D02.

### Teaching across the books

GoldieExample14 constructs a line,reflects its negative branch and translates the resulting V;Exercise1C4 gives separate transformation sketches. YM builds from|x|and transformations,thenExample9 separates intercepts,vertex and full sketch. It later couples two parameterised graphs and an inequality inExample26. In both books most retained tasks give the equation and ask a graph;source solution pictures must not be recast as learner givens.

Demands without an NSSCAS part: none.

### Difficulty and grade descriptors

C10 supports basic modulus sketches;A11 includes combined scaled/shifted form. QUAL01 still limits original graph coefficients to integers. YMTry9Q1(c) has fractional vertex5/2but integer original coefficients,whereasTry9Q2(e) has original coefficient-1/3and is outside. Example26is valid algebra for allpositivea,but its graph branch fitsQUAL01only for positive integers. No numerical grade domains are settled here.

### Pitfalls and evidential limits

YMExample8 has correct shifted graph but wrong prose sign for its horizontal shift. Fractional vertices must not be confused with fractional coefficients;allintercepts/slopes must agree with the original formula. These are source/author cautions;no examiner-confirmed modulus-specific misconception count is asserted.

### Gaps and representations

Both books strongly support equation-to-sketch. The given-graph reconstruction task is thin in the inspected books despite exam evidence. YMRevision8Q3 broadens to a two-graph root-count demonstration involving a quartic;Exercise19 overlays a parabola and|x|after a negative-root parameter condition. These are visual reasoning branches beyond oneV-sketch.

## Curve gradient, derivative notation and rational-power/chain rules

Demands: 8227:2020:M1:T8:O01:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O01:D02, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O02:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O02:D02.

### Teaching across the books

YM begins with two-sided chord/tangent interpretation,then optional first-principles work,derivative notation,power/sum rules,changed variable and explicit chain rule. Try5Q1requires two methods and an agreement check. Goldie2&3assumes this background and opens its differentiation chapter with product/quotient rules,also showing expansion as a check. An expandable product inYMdoesnot automatically assess Mathematics2product rule;an explicit product-rule instruction inGoldie does.

### Examination comparison

Directly assessed NSSCAS parts: 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q11:a, 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q11:b, 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q12:d, 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q12:b, 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q7:answer, 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q8:b, 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q3:b, 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q5:a, 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a, 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q11:answer, 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q13:b, 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q8:b, 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q11:c, 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q13:a, 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q1:answer, 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q8:a, 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q9:b, 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q8:answer.

Structural branches: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:product-polynomial-radical-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q5:a — The source supplies explicit polynomial factors and asks only for their product derivative. The exam product includes an exponential and must then impose a gradient and rearrange it logarithmically. This is easier product-rule support, not the complete fixed-point derivation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:quotient-algebraic-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q1:answer — The source and exam both differentiate an affine-over-affine rational rule using quotient subtraction and its squared denominator. The source stops at the derivative expression; the exam additionally substitutes x=-4 to return a numerical gradient. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-standard-sum-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q3:b — The source differentiates 3sin(x) directly. The exam sine-square curve additionally requires a power-chain contribution and exact evaluation at the selected crossing A. The source covers only the elementary sine term, not the whole gradient response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-composite-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q3:b — Only source Try Now 18 Q2(a) is compared: differentiate a power of sin(x) with its inner derivative. The exam adds another sine term and evaluates exactly at the inherited graph crossing. The source power-chain response is easier support without the exact-point calculation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-product-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q5:a — The source differentiates 5x exp(-2x), retaining both product terms and the affine exponential factor. The exam differentiates 6x exp(x/3), then sets a prescribed gradient and rearranges to the supplied logarithmic recurrence. The source lacks the condition/rearrangement responses. Demands without an NSSCAS part: none.

### Difficulty and grade descriptors

E08explicitly names gradient and first/second derivative notation and excludes first-principles differentiation. C08/A08permit rationalpowers and chain rule,with product/quotient not required inMaths1. Difficulty comes from rewriting,innerfunction choice and original real domains,not simply a large exponent. The source all-real-power statement must not enlarge rational-power scope.

### Pitfalls and evidential limits

YMTry7Q1prints an off-curve point but the intended-inputgradient is correct;Example10has a denominator-cleared falseparameterpair. A fourthroot(-4x)needsnonpositivexanditsfinite derivative strictnegativex. These are source/author cautions;no frequency is inferred from them. Notation preference against negativepowers is not an official marking prohibition.

### Gaps and representations

YM supplies the core introductory progression;Goldie gives advanced-rule variants and alternative expansion checks. A supplied derivative task needs no unasked reconstruction of the curve. Pointwise ordinate and derivative value must remain separate outputs.

### Proposals for step 3

Review problem sets for these exact demands: 8227:2020:M1:T8:O01:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O01:D02, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O02:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O02:D02.

Begin equivalence review with 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:numerical-gradient-constraint-chain against 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q5:a; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:tangent-normal-at-point against 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q11:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:connected-rate-inverse against 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q12:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:first-second-derivative-orders against 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q11:answer; 3 further named same-type family/part pairs are in the objective map. Carry only the selected exemplar response labels into later branch-reference decisions.

A concrete easier comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:product-polynomial-radical-derivative against 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q5:a: The source supplies explicit polynomial factors and asks only for their product derivative. The exam product includes an exponential and must then impose a gradient and rearrange it logarithmically. This is easier product-rule support, not the complete fixed-point derivation. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete harder comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:explicit-second-derivative-zero against 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q11:answer: Both require differentiating an explicit composite function twice. Goldie asks for coordinates where the second derivative vanishes, adding a zero-equation and ordinate evaluation to the exam’s two derivative formulas; its exponential product also needs the product rule, whereas the exam 9/sqrt(3-2x) uses repeated power/chain differentiation. No claim that this exponential point task is the same type as the radical derivative-only task. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete mixed comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-quotient-derivative against 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q1:answer: The source differentiates a sine/cosine quotient and simplifies using sin-squared+cos-squared=1. The exam quotient has affine algebraic factors and adds numerical evaluation at x=-4. The source adds trigonometric derivative/identity decisions; the exam adds point evaluation. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

Resolve overlapping assessed/component registry links using the accepted source-item union. The next planning stage decides recipe boundaries, marking, generated diagrams and branch references; these suggestions approve none of them.

## Tangents, normals, angle conventions and monotonic intervals

Demands: 8227:2020:M1:T8:O03:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O03:D02.

### Teaching across the books

YM separates tangent,normal,given-gradient lines,intercept geometry,positive-axis inclination and acute tangent angles. Its derivative-sign tasks progress from linear sign inequalities to disconnectedrationaldomains and extremal branchendpoints. Goldie applies the same line/stationary ideas to rational,e/lnandtrigfunctions and explicitly includes longer connected normal/intersection/area chains. Method sophistication alone doesnotchange which geometric evidence is asked.

### Examination comparison

Directly assessed NSSCAS parts: 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q11:b, 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b, 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q7:answer, 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q7:answer, 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q13:a.

Same-type textbook practice: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:tangent-normal-at-point to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q11:b.

Structural branches: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:explicit-tangent-incidence-parameter to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q7:answer — The source imposes that the tangent at unknown p to (4-x)*exp(x) passes through the origin, equating its derivative with a secant/incidence slope. The target instead supplies tangent slope -9 and recovers the quartic contact point/intercept. Unknown contact is shared, but point-incidence versus prescribed-slope equations and product-exponential versus polynomial differentiation differ. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:implicit-point-gradient-line to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b — The source GoldieExample16 derives the implicit gradient of y^3+xy=2, evaluates it at a fully given point and constructs a tangent. The target use the prior implicit derivative, recover the ordinate at x=-1 and give the normal equation. The source adds its tangent/derivative-expression response, while the target recovers a missing ordinate and changes tangent to normal. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:implicit-linked-normal-curve-sketch to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b — The source Goldie4D Q6 derives the implicit gradient of (x-6)(y+4)=2, constructs a normal at (7,-2), finds its second curve intersection and obtains asymptotes/sketch. The target use the prior implicit derivative, recover the ordinate at x=-1 and give the normal equation. The source adds connected line/intersection/representation responses. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:implicit-linked-normal-curve-sketch to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b — Only source (ii) is compared. It uses its preceding implicit derivative and a fully given point to obtain the normal. The target additionally recovers the missing ordinate from its given x value; source second intersection and sketch responses are excluded. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:parametric-regular-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q7:answer — The source Goldie4E Q1(i) returns the general quotient (dy/dt)/(dx/dt) for polynomial parameter coordinates, without a point/line condition. The target additionally locate both x-axis crossings of a parametric polynomial curve and give a tangent at each. This is easier supporting quotient construction and does not cover that target selection/evaluation response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:parametric-general-tangent-normal to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q7:answer — Both obtain dy/dx by dividing parameter derivatives and use it to form a tangent at a particular parameter. Example22 supplies t=3; the exam first solves y=t(1+t)=0 to obtain two contact parameters and must return both tangents. That contact-point selection and repeated tangent output makes the selected textbook response easier. The general tangent in ii is source scaffolding, and elimination/sketch iv is excluded. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:parametric-fixed-parameter-line to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q7:answer — The retained source Goldie4E Q2(ii)-(iii) gives a fixed parameter and requires both tangent and normal equations. The target locate both x-axis crossings of a parametric polynomial curve and give a tangent at each. The source adds two line-equation outputs and a negative-reciprocal slope; the target first locates two x-axis points. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:parametric-geometric-point-selection to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q7:answer — The source Example39 derives its trig parametric quotient, chooses the positive y-axis crossing and constructs that tangent. The target locates both x-axis crossings of polynomial parameter coordinates and constructs both tangents. Axis/sign selection and requested line multiplicity differ. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:parametric-prescribed-gradient to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q7:answer — The source Goldie4E Q11 derives the quotient, sets it equal to the prescribed slope one and solves for a point using the original coordinates/domain t>1. The target locate both x-axis crossings of a parametric polynomial curve and give a tangent at each. Searching a parameter from a slope differs from selecting axis crossings or evaluating at an already known parameter. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:implicit-given-point-gradient to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b — The source Example29 gives both coordinates and asks only for the implicit gradient. The target use the prior implicit derivative, recover the ordinate at x=-1 and give the normal equation. The source omits recovery of the missing coordinate and construction of the negative-reciprocal normal line. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-sum-differentiation to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q11:b — Example1 returns single-term power derivatives (including roots, reciprocals and a constant). The target must additionally differentiate the explicit power-sum curve and construct its normal at (4,20). The drill is easier derivative-rule preparation; it omits that application/condition and response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-sum-differentiation to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q7:answer — Example1 returns single-term power derivatives (including roots, reciprocals and a constant). The target must additionally use prescribed tangent slope -9 to find an unknown quartic contact and line intercept. The drill is easier derivative-rule preparation; it omits that application/condition and response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:derivative-constraint-parameters to 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q13:a — Example9 begins with y=ax^2+bx and two gradient conditions, so the learner differentiates and solves two simultaneous coefficient equations. The target has its first derivative given and just one unknown coefficient after converting a normal slope to the tangent slope. The source is harder in derivative construction and two-parameter recovery; the target normal conversion remains a distinct condition where present. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:tangent-normal-at-point to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b — Example12 provides the full point on an explicit reciprocal curve and asks for its normal. The target already has its implicit derivative but supplies only x=-1, so the learner must first recover the ordinate from the implicit relation. The source is easier in point selection and implicit-curve use. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:tangent-normal-at-point to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q7:answer — Example11 constructs one tangent at a prescribed x on an explicit quadratic. The target must differentiate parametric coordinates, solve for both x-axis contacts and construct two tangents. The source is easier fixed-contact, single-line preparation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:tangent-triangle-area-chain to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b — The book differentiates an explicit rational curve and uses a supplied full point to form a tangent. The exam supplies the implicit derivative but requires recovering the point and taking the negative reciprocal for a normal. Different setup and perpendicular-gradient decisions make the ordering mixed; the book triangle-area part is excluded. (mixed).

Demands without an NSSCAS part: 8227:2020:M1:T8:O03:D02.

### Difficulty and grade descriptors

E08already names tangents,normals,increasing/decreasing functions;C08/A08retain linked applications. Harder tasks infer a point from intersections,distinguish maximalinterval from strictderivativesignset,or use a normal later. Horizontal/vertical exceptions and connected domaincomponents matter more than coefficient size.

### Pitfalls and evidential limits

YM13’swrongtangent/normal lines stillpass through the correctpoint;direction checks are essential. Try12Q4has literalstem/keyconflict. Example18’srationalmonotonicity must be read per connectedcomponent;Try18Q2has an isolatedzerogradient without negativegradient. These are registered source cautions,not claims that an automated image view provescorrect marking.

### Gaps and representations

Both books support analyticlines;YM offers explicit inclination and maximal-domain branches. An isolatedzero derivative doesnotautomaticallybreak strictincrease. Source given versus solution graphs cannot be silently interchanged.

A concrete harder comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:implicit-linked-normal-curve-sketch against 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q10:b: The source Goldie4D Q6 derives the implicit gradient of (x-6)(y+4)=2, constructs a normal at (7,-2), finds its second curve intersection and obtains asymptotes/sketch. The target instead supplies tangent slope -9 and recovers the quartic contact point/intercept. Unknown contact is shared, but point-incidence versus prescribed-slope equations and product-exponential versus polynomial differentiation differ. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

## Signed direct/connected rates and motion interpretation

Demands: 8227:2020:M1:T8:O03:D03, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O03:D04.

### Teaching across the books

YM distinguishes directtime models,forwardchainrates,inverse rates and inferredpositions,then displacement/velocity/acceleration. Goldie’s exponentialriverheight and later advancedcalculus contexts apply different standard derivatives to the same rate logic. A geometryformula supplied in the task is not an independently assessed modelderivation;atankdepth relation that must be derived is.

### Examination comparison

Directly assessed NSSCAS parts: 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q12:b, 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a, 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:a, 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q11:c, 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q8:answer.

Same-type textbook practice: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:connected-rate-inverse to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q12:b.

Structural branches: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-standard-sum-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:a — The source asks directly for the derivative of e^x. The exam exponential has a nonunit negative inner coefficient and the learner must interpret velocity-to-acceleration. The source omits both the inner factor and the physical rate interpretation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-standard-sum-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q11:c — The source asks for the derivative of e^x. The decay exam adds a negative scaled time argument, evaluation at t=3, context units and the positive magnitude of a decrease. None of those extra decisions is assessed in the source single-rule response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-composite-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:a — The source differentiates a scaled decreasing exponential. The exam asks for acceleration from its exponential velocity; that physical derivative choice and units are additional to the explicitly cued source differentiation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-composite-derivative to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q11:c — The source differentiates a scaled decreasing affine exponential symbolically. The exam additionally evaluates at a given time, interprets the signed derivative as a positive decrease magnitude and supplies the rate units. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:trapezium-stationary-preparation to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — Source (a) finds a minimum abscissa of x-2ln(x), using a logarithmic derivative and its positive domain. The exam finds the maximum time of a supplied quadratic height model. The source adds a reciprocal derivative/denominator step; the exam interprets the root as time. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:explicit-root-arc-investigation to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — Both find the positive input at a stated maximum by setting the derivative to zero. The source must establish a product/inner-root derivative and respect its finite arc; the ball-height exam uses a quadratic with a linear derivative and interprets the root as time. The source adds the product/chain and radical-domain work. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-sum-differentiation to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q12:b — Example1 returns single-term power derivatives (including roots, reciprocals and a constant). The target must additionally invert the chain rate of the already established cone volume V=3*pi*h^3/25 at h=15 and dV/dt=36 to recover dh/dt. The drill is easier derivative-rule preparation; it omits that application/condition and response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-sum-differentiation to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — Example1 returns single-term power derivatives (including roots, reciprocals and a constant). The target must additionally differentiate the given height 80t-16t^2 and find the time of greatest height. The drill is easier derivative-rule preparation; it omits that application/condition and response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-sum-differentiation to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q8:answer — Example1 returns single-term power derivatives (including roots, reciprocals and a constant). The target must additionally find the physically admissible rest time from v=2t-11+6/(t+1), then differentiate v and evaluate its acceleration at rest. The drill is easier derivative-rule preparation; it omits that application/condition and response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-chain-rule to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q8:answer — Try Now6(d) differentiates a reciprocal of a cubic and stops at the general derivative. The target has an affine reciprocal velocity term but additionally solves v=0, rejects the inadmissible time and evaluates acceleration there. This is easier supporting reciprocal-chain differentiation, not the full rest-time task. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:direct-time-rate to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q11:c — Example19 differentiates a supplied cubic volume-time formula and evaluates its signed rate at a stated time. The target uses exponential decay and explicitly requests a positive rate of decrease. The source is easier power-sum time differentiation; exponential differentiation and decrease-sign interpretation are additional target decisions. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-derivative-chain to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — Example21 differentiates a given displacement formula twice and evaluates at the supplied time t=1. The target additionally differentiate the given height 80t-16t^2 and find the time of greatest height. The source is easier forward evaluation and does not choose the unknown event time from a zero derivative/velocity condition. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-derivative-chain to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q8:answer — Example21 differentiates a given displacement formula twice and evaluates at the supplied time t=1. The target additionally find the physically admissible rest time from v=2t-11+6/(t+1), then differentiate v and evaluate its acceleration at rest. The source is easier forward evaluation and does not choose the unknown event time from a zero derivative/velocity condition. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:connected-rate-forward to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q12:b — Example22 differentiates A=pi*r^2 and multiplies the given dr/dt to obtain dA/dt at r=10. The target differentiates the established cubic cone relation and divides the given dV/dt by dV/dh to recover dh/dt. The source is easier forward rate propagation with a quadratic relation, rather than inverse rate recovery. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:connected-rate-inverse to 8227:EXAM:2025:P2:Q11:c — The book a-gradient differentiates a supplied explicit curve and evaluates its gradient at a given x. The exam differentiates a time-decay model and interprets the signed derivative as a positive rate of decrease with units. The rate interpretation is absent from the selected source gradient response. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:stationary-classify-sketch to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — The source returns both cubic stationary coordinates, their natures and a sketch. The isolated exam part interprets a quadratic motion rule and returns just the time of maximum height. The source adds classification/graph responses; the exam adds the time/velocity interpretation. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:stationary-analytic-targets to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — Example29 differentiates a quartic, finds both stationary coordinates and classifies an ordinary minimum and a stationary inflexion, requiring more than a nonzero second derivative test. The target differentiate the given height 80t-16t^2 and find the time of greatest height. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:stationary-analytic-targets to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — Only Try Now29 Q1(b) stationary coordinates are compared. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:optimise-split-wire-loops to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — The source derives its area quadratic from a one wire split into a square perimeter and circular circumference and returns a positive-quadratic total area and its stationary square side; the printed maximise sentence is defective. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:optimise-rectangle-under-line to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a — The source derives its area quadratic from a rectangle under the line through two supplied intercepts and returns A=12x-3x^2/4; stationary area and nature. The exam starts with its quadratic height already supplied and asks only for the maximum time. The source adds geometric elimination. (harder).

Demands without an NSSCAS part: none.

### Difficulty and grade descriptors

E08/C08/A08all include rates and motion. Difficulty increases through signedinputrate,inverse derivative/nonzerorateconditions and geometrybeforecalculus. Large source-model velocities are not evidence of real-world plausibility or a higher grade.

### Pitfalls and evidential limits

YMTry22Q1prints cm/sinstead ofcm²/s;Example24needs a nonzero cancellation qualification. Negativevelocity is not speed,and negativeacceleration alone is not always deceleration. These source/author distinctions are not automatically examiner-evidenced misconceptions;ERR03concerns initialconditions more broadly in linked motion work.

### Gaps and representations

Both books supply time/ratecontexts;YM systematically separates forward and inversechains. Model quantities have different unitpowers. A decrease should remain a signedrate unless the prompt explicitly requests its positivemagnitude. Context realism and mathematical validity are separate checks.

## Stationary classification, inflexions and geometry-specific optimisation

Demands: 8227:2020:M1:T8:O04:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O04:D02.

### Teaching across the books

YM separates locate/nature/sketch chains,stationaryinflexions,abscissa-only/given-derivative tasks and inverse graphconstraints before optimisationmodels. It explicitly changes objective/constraint between fixedvolume-area and fixedsurface-volume. Goldie extends stationary analysis to rational,e/lnandtrigfunctions and connected tangent/intersectiongeometry. Both permit valid alternative naturetests where the questiondoesnotprescribeone.

### Difficulty and grade descriptors

C08/A08explicitly locate and distinguish maximum,minimumandinflexion. E08supplies applicationbackgroundwithoutrestatingallclassification. Difficulty grows with inconclusivesecondderivative,domainfiltering,hidden graphconstraints and derivingthe rightgeometryobjective. Gradeparameterdomains waitforstep3.

### Pitfalls and evidential limits

YM27copies a wrongcoordinate sentence beside a correctgraph;29needs more thanf″=0;Try31Q4uses touchingrootDatgraphlevel. Example34misnames a minimumasmaximum;cone radiusfalseleadswerecleared;some keyblocks are swapped under wrongheadings. These aresource defects,cautions and clearedleads,not interchangeablelearner errors.

### Gaps and representations

Textbook modelbreadth goes beyond a generic finalquadratic derivative:closedcuboid,open tank,cylinder,inscribedcone,Lshape,curvedplate,sharedfences and foldedcardhave different constraints. Optimisingthegradientfunction inRevision6Q4changes the target entirely. A source’sunasked sketchornaturecheck is not an assessed response.

### Proposals for step 3

Review problem sets for these exact demands: 8227:2020:M1:T8:O04:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T8:O04:D02.

Begin equivalence review with 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:stationary-classify-sketch against 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q9:c; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:stationary-classify-sketch against 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q10:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:optimise-closed-cuboid-area against 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q9:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:optimise-closed-cuboid-area against 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q9:c; 3 further named same-type family/part pairs are in the objective map. Carry only the selected exemplar response labels into later branch-reference decisions.

A concrete easier comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:quotient-algebraic-derivative against 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q5:a: The source differentiates an explicit algebraic quotient and returns its derivative. The exam logarithmic quotient additionally requires the ln derivative, solving derivative zero with domain checks, and recovering the stationary ordinate. The source supports quotient ordering only. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete harder comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:explicit-root-arc-investigation against 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a: Both find the positive input at a stated maximum by setting the derivative to zero. The source must establish a product/inner-root derivative and respect its finite arc; the ball-height exam uses a quadratic with a linear derivative and interprets the root as time. The source adds the product/chain and radical-domain work. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete mixed comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:trapezium-stationary-preparation against 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q10:a: Source (a) finds a minimum abscissa of x-2ln(x), using a logarithmic derivative and its positive domain. The exam finds the maximum time of a supplied quadratic height model. The source adds a reciprocal derivative/denominator step; the exam interprets the root as time. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

## Reverse differentiation, power primitives and fixed-curve conditions

Demands: 8227:2020:M1:T9:O01:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T9:O01:D02, 8227:2020:M1:T9:O02:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T9:O02:D02.

### Teaching across the books

YMstartswithreversearrows/generalcurvefamilies,thenrationalpowers,expanded/simplifiedintegrandsandlinear-chainreversal. Itdistinguishesarbitraryconstantfromparameterk,onepointfromgeometricallyinferredconditions,andtwoconstantsforsecond-derivative recovery. Goldie2&3assumesmostpowerbackgroundbutretainsordinaryprimitive/pointrecoveryinsidetheotherwiselargelyadditionalseparable-ODEchapter. Itsone-constantpromptdoesnotrequiregeneralODEsolving.

### Examination comparison

Directly assessed NSSCAS parts: 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q4:a, 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q4:b, 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a, 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q13:answer, 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q14:b, 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q12:answer, 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a, 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q13:b, 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q14:answer, 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q5:b, 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q11:c, 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q8:b.

Structural branches: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-reciprocal-definite-logform to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q5:b — An affine reciprocal log term remains after the exam reuses the polynomial division from (a), but the answer must include the polynomial primitive and C on each original domain component. The source supplies the reciprocal-linear form directly and asks for one exact proper definite log value. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-trig-primitives to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The source chooses standard affine trig primitives and the inner scale/sign, without evaluating an unknown upper bound, adding the polynomial integral and proving a fixed-point rearrangement. Those are additional target decisions. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-trig-definite to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The source evaluates a supplied trig integral with known exact endpoints. The exam integrates an additional polynomial term to an unknown upper limit, imposes the given total and rearranges for a cube-root iteration. Fixed-endpoint evaluation is easier supporting practice. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:log-derivative-division-integral-chain to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q5:b — Both reuse a polynomial quotient/remainder to separate polynomial and reciprocal-linear terms. The source then proves an exact definite value using endpoints; the exam asks for an indefinite primitive with C and the original denominator hole, following its prior division part. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-area-given-region to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a — The source Example10 reads a fixed under-curve region and chooses a reciprocal-affine logarithmic primitive. The exam evaluate the pictured positive polynomial area under 9-x^2 between the coordinate axes. The boundary-to-integral decision is shared, but the polynomial power rule replaces the source logarithm rule. These are different integrand branches, with no uniform difficulty ordering. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-trig-washer-volume to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q14:b — The source x-axis disc requires expanding (tan(x)+cos(x))^2, integrating its sine cross term and reusing a preceding trig identity. The exam rotate the sqrt(3x+2) region about x, square the radius to 3x+2 and use the preceding axis intercepts. The source adds identity/cross-term work. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:rational-expanded-power-log-primitive to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q5:b — The source expands (4-x)^2/x^2 into negative powers, a reciprocal and a constant before indefinite integration. The exam inherits a polynomial quotient/remainder from the preceding part and integrates positive powers plus an affine reciprocal. Both separate power and logarithm primitives, but expansion versus inherited division and negative versus positive powers differ. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-area-sign-and-shift to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a — Goldie8B Q3(i) finds total area across both sides of an axis crossing for6x(x^2+1)^3, reversing the negative-side sign. The target has a wholly nonnegative parabola and one interval. The source is harder in sign splitting and its higher finite power expansion; its printed substitution is not silently required by8227. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-area-sign-and-shift to 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q14:answer — Goldie8B Q5 forms upper-line minus root-curve area from the y-axis to their crossing. The target has a horizontal line above a quadratic between two pictured crossings. The source is harder in fractional-power integration, while the given diagram supplies its bounds; the vertical boundary versus two-crossing enclosure remains an explicit structural difference. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:rational-linear-numerator-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q5:b — The source divides an affine numerator by an affine denominator before a finite exact evaluation. The exam divides a cubic by a linear denominator, using earlier recovered coefficients, and returns a quadratic polynomial plus a logarithm and an arbitrary constant. The higher-degree division and longer primitive make the exam form more demanding; the source fixed endpoints remain explicit. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:numerical-supplied-map-chain to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The exam obtains its cube-root map by integrating a mixed polynomial/trig expression and imposing an unknown endpoint integral condition. Begin equivalence review with 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:curve-first-derivative-point against 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q13:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:curve-first-derivative-point against 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q8:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:area-positive-axis-region against 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:area-two-curves against 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q14:answer; 4 further named same-type family/part pairs are in the objective map. Carry only the selected exemplar response labels into later branch-reference decisions.

A concrete easier comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-reciprocal-definite-logform against 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q5:b: An affine reciprocal log term remains after the exam reuses the polynomial division from (a), but the answer must include the polynomial primitive and C on each original domain component. The source supplies the reciprocal-linear form directly and asks for one exact proper definite log value. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete harder comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:log-derivative-division-integral-chain against 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q5:b: Both reuse a polynomial quotient/remainder to separate polynomial and reciprocal-linear terms. The source then proves an exact definite value using endpoints; the exam asks for an indefinite primitive with C and the original denominator hole, following its prior division part. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete mixed comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-area-given-region against 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a: The source Example10 reads a fixed under-curve region and chooses a reciprocal-affine logarithmic primitive. The exam evaluate the pictured positive polynomial area under 9-x^2 between the coordinate axes. The boundary-to-integral decision is shared, but the polynomial power rule replaces the source logarithm rule. These are different integrand branches, with no uniform difficulty ordering. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

(mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:product-reversal-curve-conversion-chain to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q5:b — Only source (ii), using its preceding derivative of (x+3)exp(-x), is compared. It recognises the negative of the target integrand and gives an indefinite primitive. The exam uses a preceding derivative to evaluate finite logarithmic endpoints. The other stationary/conversion source responses are excluded. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:affine-reciprocal-proper-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q8:answer — The source evaluates a proper reciprocal-affine integral with known endpoints. The exam puts the same positive parameter into both endpoints and requires solving the resulting logarithmic ratio for that parameter. Forward exact evaluation is easier supporting practice and omits inverse-bound recovery. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:finite-trig-product-integration to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q5:b — Example11 evaluates one cos(x)*sin^2(x) integral at exact special-angle endpoints. The source is easier single-product preparation; its printed general-substitution method is not an additional prescribed8227 demand. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:finite-trig-product-integration to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q6:a — Example11 evaluates one cos(x)*sin^2(x) integral at exact special-angle endpoints. The source is easier single-product preparation; its printed general-substitution method is not an additional prescribed8227 demand. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-area-sign-and-shift to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a — Goldie8B Q3(i) finds total area across both sides of an axis crossing for6x(x^2+1)^3, reversing the negative-side sign. The target has a wholly nonnegative parabola and one interval. The source is harder in sign splitting and its higher finite power expansion; its printed substitution is not silently required by8227. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-area-sign-and-shift to 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q14:answer — Goldie8B Q5 forms upper-line minus root-curve area from the y-axis to their crossing. The target has a horizontal line above a quadratic between two pictured crossings. The source is harder in fractional-power integration, while the given diagram supplies its bounds; the vertical boundary versus two-crossing enclosure remains an explicit structural difference. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:rational-linear-numerator-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q2:answer — The source first reduces 9x/(3x-8) to a constant plus a reciprocal-linear term and integrates both over fixed endpoints. The exam is a pure reciprocal-linear integral with no division step; the source adds the constant contribution and its endpoint difference. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:logarithm-equation-domain-filter to 8227:EXAM:2022:P2:Q8:answer — The book solves a supplied logarithmic equation and gives x in terms of e. The exam first integrates, constructs the ratio-of-integrals equation and handles the admissible parameter before recovering it. The book is a narrower equation-solving branch. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:numerical-supplied-map-chain to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The exam obtains its cube-root map by integrating a mixed polynomial/trig expression and imposing an unknown endpoint integral condition. The source begins with a curve intersection and requires only its algebraic square-root rearrangement. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:numerical-integral-bound-chain to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The source integrates an exponential-plus-constant function to unknown a, applies the stated value and proves a logarithmic map. The exam integrates a polynomial/sine/cosine sum and derives a cube-root map. Both establish the unknown-bound condition, but the primitives, admissible inverse and domain justification differ. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:numerical-region-area-chain to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The source first interprets the region between y=1 and y=exp(-x), integrates its area and imposes area=1 before its exponential map. The exam supplies a definite-integral equality directly and derives a cube-root map after polynomial/trig integration. Source geometric setup and exam richer primitives/inverse recovery are different decisions. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-antiderivative-family to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q4:b — The source stops at an indefinite power primitive. The exam must use the preceding velocity, evaluate its accumulated displacement from zero to three seconds and interpret the position. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-antiderivative-family to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a — The source integrates a supplied polynomial. The exam must read the bounded region, select the intercept limits and evaluate its area rather than return a general primitive. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-antiderivative-family to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q14:b — The source integrates a sum of powers. The exam must first choose the squared rotation radius, use the negative intercept and include pi in a volume integral. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-antiderivative-family to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The source practises only the polynomial primitive in the exam integrand. The exam additionally integrates sine/cosine terms, applies the unknown upper bound and proves a fixed-point rearrangement. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-antiderivative-family to 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q14:answer — The source returns an indefinite polynomial primitive. The exam must choose line minus parabola on the pictured interval and calculate their enclosed area. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:power-antiderivative-family to 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q11:c — The source handles the positive and negative powers of the eventual difference integrand. The exam must choose parabola minus reciprocal-square curve and apply the positive intersection limits to return area. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:linear-power-antiderivative to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q12:answer — The source directly integrates a negative power of a nonconstant affine expression with its reverse-chain coefficient and arbitrary constant. The exam uses the same affine negative-power primitive but must form an area, apply the lower and variable upper bounds, then justify convergence as t tends to infinity. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:proper-power-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q4:b — The source evaluates a supplied finite polynomial integral. The exam must identify the previously obtained velocity as the displacement integrand and interpret the result in metres. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:proper-power-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a — The source supplies integrand and limits explicitly; the exam first translates the shaded region and intercepts into the correct definite area integral. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:proper-power-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:b — The source supplies a finite integral in one variable. The exam must choose rotation about y, express the squared radius in y, select its y-limits and include pi. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:proper-power-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q12:answer — The source evaluates fixed finite limits. The exam must integrate an affine reciprocal square to a variable upper bound and prove a finite limiting area; endpoint subtraction alone does not establish convergence. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:proper-power-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q7:a — The source applies a power primitive at fixed limits. The exam also needs trig primitives, an unknown upper bound and algebraic rearrangement of the resulting equation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:proper-power-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q14:answer — The source has a supplied integrand and limits. The exam must form upper line minus lower parabola from the diagram before the finite power evaluation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:proper-power-definite-integral to 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q11:c — The source evaluates a sum of positive/negative powers over supplied finite limits. The exam must identify the upper curve and use the positive intersections from the previous part to form the area difference. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:simple-improper-limits to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q12:answer — Example26 evaluates the explicit integral of1/x^2 from1 to infinity using a finite upper cutoff and its limit. The target supplies a geometric region for an affine reciprocal-square curve and requires forming that area integral before taking the same kind of tail limit. The source is easier: identity affine coefficients and an already stated integral, with no diagram-to-integral formation. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:area-negative-and-sign-split to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a — The source Example18 must identify a zero inside [0,4], split the x(x-3) region there, reverse the negative piece and add positive areas. The exam region 9-x^2 on [0,3] stays nonnegative and uses one integral. Sign-change detection and absolute area addition are additional source decisions. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:area-horizontal-inverse-branch to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a — The source shaded region is bounded horizontally between y=1 and y=3, so it must select x=+sqrt(y) from the plotted branch and integrate horizontal width. The exam already supplies y=9-x^2 for a direct positive vertical-strip integral. Source branch inversion and strip-variable choice are extra relative to that direct exam route. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:area-two-curves to 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q11:c — The source must solve its line/parabola intersections and integrate a polynomial gap. The exam inherits its earlier intersection work but must integrate a gap containing a reciprocal-square term. The source adds independent bound recovery, while the target adds a negative-power boundary. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:area-tangent-normal-boundaries to 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q14:answer — The source Example23 reconstructs the normal-line/axis enclosure from given curve points and uses trapezium-minus-curve area on its actual interval. The exam supplies its bounding line and requires only the line/parabola intersection gap. Normal-boundary geometry and the enclosing trapezium are additional source decisions. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:volume-x-disc to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:b — The source Example31 rotates about x and squares its given y(x) radius. The exam rotates about y and must obtain x^2=9-y and y-limits instead. Choosing the other coordinate axis changes the radius expression and integration variable; neither may inherit the other axis unchanged. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:volume-x-disc to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q14:b — The source Example31 rotates y=x^2 about x and integrates a fourth-power squared radius using stated vertical bounds. The exam rotates y=sqrt(3x+2) about x, uses the preceding intercepts and simplifies its squared radius to an affine expression. The disc model is shared; exponent simplification and bound preparation differ. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:volume-x-disc to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q6:b — The source forms an x-axis disc model and integrates the square of an algebraic radius. The exam must additionally expand a cosine-plus-secant square and reuse its preceding exact trig integral. The source is easier model/whole-radius-squaring practice and does not cover those trig decisions. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:volume-y-disc-inverse to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q14:b — The source rotates about y and recovers x^2 from y=x^2+1 before integrating in y. The exam rotate the sqrt(3x+2) region about x, square the radius to 3x+2 and use the preceding axis intercepts. The axis/radius expression and primitive preparation differ, so the selected source is an axis-choice branch rather than a same-type instance. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:volume-y-disc-inverse to 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q6:b — The source rotates about y and recovers x^2 from y=x^2+1 before integrating in y. The exam rotate a cosine-plus-secant region about x, expand the squared trig sum and reuse the preceding exact integral. The axis/radius expression and primitive preparation differ, so the selected source is an axis-choice branch rather than a same-type instance. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-two-integration-conditions to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q4:b — The selected book displacement response integrates available velocity and uses a nonzero initial displacement to give the complete displacement function. Exam b uses the velocity from a, starts at the origin, and returns the numerical distance after 3 seconds. Recovering a full function/constant and interpreting a numerical distance are different response requirements, not merely different coefficients. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-two-integration-conditions to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:b — The source starts with linear acceleration and must recover both velocity and displacement using initial conditions. The exam supplies a positive exponential velocity and asks for one finite-interval distance. Source reconstruction/constant decisions and target exponential primitive differ. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-vertical-events to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:b — Part (c-i) integrates the previously recovered v=10t+8 and fixes s(0)=0 at the shaft top; (c-ii) evaluates that displacement at the stated 4 s to give depth. The target integrates its supplied positive velocity from the start to a stated 2 s. Both give distance with no reversal or event-time solving. The source’s explicit displacement expression is the intermediate indefinite-integral route to the same numeric motion response, not a requirement to solve the unrelated top event in Example 39. D1 correction: the polynomial power-rule primitive is easier than the target exponential primitive, whose reciprocal chain factor is an assessed ingredient. It is a structural branch, not same type. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-rest-and-bounded-speed to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q4:b — The source Example38 starts from a quadratic velocity and asks for initial velocity, acceleration, maximum velocity, first-two-seconds distance and next rest time. The target use the previous positive velocity to find distance from O after three seconds. Those extra derivative/extremum/rest responses stay explicit; the selected distance-only exception is separate. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-rest-and-bounded-speed to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:b — Selected Example 38(d) asks the distance travelled in the first 2 s from given v=12t-t²; v is nonnegative on 0..2 and its single definite integral gives 64/3 m. The target uses the same fixed interval, given one-signed velocity, motion interpretation and numeric distance response. The source’s acceleration, maximum and next-rest parts are not selected. D1 correction: the polynomial power-rule primitive is easier than the target exponential primitive, whose reciprocal chain factor is an assessed ingredient. It is a structural branch, not same type. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-distance-with-reversal to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q4:b — The source Exercise7 Q7 must recover quadratic velocity, solve its positive rest time and split the requested distance interval at its direction reversal. The target use the previous positive velocity to find distance from O after three seconds. The source adds rest-root and absolute-distance-piece decisions that the target does not require. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-distance-with-reversal to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:b — The selected part (c) asks total distance on 0..3 after the velocity/rest event have been recovered. It must recognise the interior reversal at 2 and add |integral 0..2v|+|integral 2..3 v|. The target supplies v=30exp(-t/2)>0 and needs only integral 0..2v. This is a mixed motion-distance branch: the source adds the sign-change split but omits the target exponential primitive; the unrelated maximum/velocity at 3 responses are not compared. (mixed); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:motion-positive-velocity-interval to 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:b — The source starts with a positive rational velocity, obtains initial acceleration and evaluates its distance on [3,6]. The target starts with positive exponential velocity and asks only for distance on [0,2]. The source adds the initial derivative response and uses an affine negative-power primitive instead of an exponential one. (mixed).

### Proposals for step 3

Review problem sets for these exact demands: 8227:2020:M1:T9:O03:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T9:O03:D02.

Begin equivalence review with 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-reciprocal-definite-logform against 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q2:answer; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:log-derivative-division-integral-chain against 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q2:answer; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:affine-reciprocal-proper-definite-integral against 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q2:answer; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:area-positive-axis-region against 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q9:a; 4 further named same-type family/part pairs are in the objective map. Carry only the selected exemplar response labels into later branch-reference decisions.

A concrete easier comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-exponential-primitives against 8227:EXAM:2023:P2:Q9:b: The same affine exponential reverse derivative is needed, but the exam first interprets a positive velocity as distance rate and evaluates bounds 0 to 2 in metres; the family returns an indefinite primitive with C. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete harder comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:elementary-trig-washer-volume against 8227:EXAM:2024:P2:Q6:b: Both rotate an axis-bounded trig-sum region about x, expand the whole squared radius and reuse a preceding square-sum exact integral. The source radius tan(x)+cos(x) leaves the cross term 2sin(x), whereas the target cos(x)+sec(x) leaves the constant cross term 2. This cross-term primitive differs while the connected volume setup is retained. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

A concrete mixed comparison is 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:given-derivative-product-reversal against 8227:EXAM:2021:P2:Q5:b: The source first differentiates x*cos(x), rearranges to isolate the integral of x*sin(x), then gives an indefinite primitive. The isolated exam part inherits the already calculated derivative of x*ln(x)-x and evaluates definite endpoints. The source adds derivative/rearrangement work; the exam adds endpoint evaluation. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.

## Positive-integer expansions, selection and coefficient constraints

Demands: 8227:2020:M1:T2:O01:D01, 8227:2020:M1:T2:O01:D02, 8227:2020:M1:T2:O01:D03.

### Teaching across the books

YM develops repeated-product/Pascal investigations before full expansions,ordered leading terms,general-term selection,Laurent powers and product/parameter constraints. It separates factorial notation from the theorem. Its Example51adds two expansions;39and47multiply them. The retained Goldie2&3source assumes this Mathematics1background and supplies only small algebraic facets such as polynomial multiplication;its excluded generalised-binomial material is not a substitute for8227positive-integer scope. The census records that asymmetry rather than inventing a parallel Goldie progression.

### Examination comparison

Directly assessed NSSCAS parts: 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q1:a, 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q1:b, 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q3:answer, 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:a, 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:b, 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q3:answer, 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q2:answer.

Same-type textbook practice: 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-direct-expansion to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q1:a; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-direct-expansion to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:a; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-chosen-term to 8227:EXAM:2024:P1:Q3:answer; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-parameter-coefficients to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q1:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-parameter-coefficients to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:b; 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-parameter-from-aligned-sum-coefficient to 8227:EXAM:2022:P1:Q3:answer.

The target requests the first three terms of a fourth/sixth power with a symbolic parameter and needs the corresponding binomial coefficients. The source is easier mixed-term/sign preparation, not higher-power coefficient selection. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:algebra-binomial-square-support to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:a — Example1(a) uses (u-v)^2=u^2-2uv+v^2 to expand a square with fractional-power terms. The target requests the first three terms of a fourth/sixth power with a symbolic parameter and needs the corresponding binomial coefficients. The source is easier mixed-term/sign preparation, not higher-power coefficient selection. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-pascal-investigation to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q1:a — The source investigation requires actual repeated multiplication of several powers, constructs coefficient rows and explains their symmetry/recursion/generalisation limits. The exam simply returns three terms of one binomial and does not require that pattern evidence. The source adds explicit multiplication, multiple expansions and explanatory responses. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-pascal-investigation to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:a — The source investigation requires actual repeated multiplication of several powers, constructs coefficient rows and explains their symmetry/recursion/generalisation limits. The exam simply returns three terms of one binomial and does not require that pattern evidence. The source adds explicit multiplication, multiple expansions and explanatory responses. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-composite-base-collection to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q1:a — The source first groups its trinomial as 1+x(2-3x), expands the outer power and collects all contributions through x^3. The exam has a genuine affine binomial and needs only its first three terms. The source adds nested total-degree bookkeeping and one further degree, so it is a harder expansion branch. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-composite-base-collection to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:a — The source first groups its trinomial as 1+x(2-3x), expands the outer power and collects all contributions through x^3. The exam has a genuine affine binomial and needs only its first three terms. The source adds nested total-degree bookkeeping and one further degree, so it is a harder expansion branch. (harder); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-product-coefficient to 8227:EXAM:2021:P1:Q1:b — The source multiplies the prepared coefficient series by a known polynomial and combines every degree pair yielding the requested coefficient. The exam additionally has an unknown scale inside the binomial, sets the resulting coefficient to its prescribed value and solves/filter the parameter equation. The source is easier known-coefficient assembly support. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-product-coefficient to 8227:EXAM:2023:P1:Q4:b — The source multiplies the prepared coefficient series by a known polynomial and combines every degree pair yielding the requested coefficient. The exam additionally has an unknown scale inside the binomial, sets the resulting coefficient to its prescribed value and solves/filter the parameter equation. The source is easier known-coefficient assembly support. (easier); 8227:TEXTBOOK-FAMILY:binomial-parameter-from-aligned-sum-coefficient to 8227:EXAM:2025:P1:Q2:answer — Both extract matching-degree coefficients from separate expansions and add/subtract them before imposing a parameter condition. The source equation is cubic in a and needs a real cube root. The exam coefficients and target yield a linear equation in a. The source is the harder nonlinear-recovery branch; subtraction rather than addition must still be preserved in the exam condition. (harder).

Demands without an NSSCAS part: none.

### Difficulty and grade descriptors

GradeE02,C02andA02all retain positive-integern,and exclude required knowledge of greatest term/properties of coefficients. The difficulty difference lies in ordering,total-degree collection,allcontributing pairs and parameter filtering. Investigation of row patterns is labelled textbook-derived support,not newly required syllabus scope. QUAL03is checked on actual outer powers,not fractional inner coefficients.

### Pitfalls and evidential limits

YM37has truncated/sign-wrong keys;Try46Q1has a wrong-variable independent-term prompt;Try47Q1needs an originally higher term because its multiplier is reciprocal. These recorded source defects matter more than agreement with a short key. The textbook warns through worked sequencing against omitted scale/sign factors;no specific examiner-frequency claim for each is made here.

### Gaps and representations

YM offers a much broader positive-integer progression than the retained Goldiebook. A position in original factor order can differ from descending-variable order;constant term and coefficient are not the same output. Book patterns beyond the prescribed knowledge remain supporting evidence. No textbook family count estimates future exam variety.

The exam simply returns three terms of one binomial and does not require that pattern evidence. The source adds explicit multiplication, multiple expansions and explanatory responses. Use this difference when deciding scaffolding and connected responses; the complete ordered comparison list is in the objective map.
